   def test_wdogload():
       print("Testing WDOGLOAD register")
       # Test writing a value
       test_value = 0x12345678
       dev.bank.regs.wdogload = test_value
       stest.expect_equal(dev.bank.regs.wdogload, test_value)
       # Test writing zero
       dev.bank.regs.wdogload = 0
       stest.expect_equal(dev.bank.regs.wdogload, 0)
       # Test writing maximum value
       dev.bank.regs.wdogload = 0xFFFFFFFF
       stest.expect_equal(dev.bank.regs.wdogload, 0xFFFFFFFF)
   # Test WDOGVALUE register read-only
   def test_wdogvalue():
       print("Testing WDOGVALUE register")
       # Set load value first
       dev.bank.regs.wdogload = 0x1000
       stest.expect_equal(dev.bank.regs.wdogvalue, 0x1000)
       # Try to write (should not change)
       try:
           dev.bank.regs.wdogvalue = 0x12345678
           # If we get here, the write succeeded, which is incorrect
           stest.expect_equal(dev.bank.regs.wdogvalue, 0x1000)  # Should still be original value
       except:
           # Write failed as expected
           pass

       print("Testing WDOGCONTROL register")
       # Test initial value

       print(f"Initial WDOGCONTROL value: 0x{initial_value:08x}")
       # Test setting interrupt enable
       dev.bank.regs.wdogcontrol = 0x1  # int_en = 1
       stest.expect_equal(dev.bank.regs.wdogcontrol & 0x1, 0x1)
       # Test setting reset enable
       dev.bank.regs.wdogcontrol = 0x2  # res_en = 1
       stest.expect_equal(dev.bank.regs.wdogcontrol & 0x2, 0x2)
       # Test setting both
       dev.bank.regs.wdogcontrol = 0x3  # int_en = 1, res_en = 1
       stest.expect_equal(dev.bank.regs.wdogcontrol, 0x3)

       dev.bank.regs.wdogintclr = 0xFFFFFFFF

   # Create an instance of the device to test

   def test_initial_lock_state():

       # This test will fail until we implement the lock functionality
       # lock_state = dev.bank.regs.wdoglock
       # We might need to check a separate attribute or status indicator
   def test_write_protection_when_locked():
       print("Testing write protection when locked")
       # Device should be locked by default
       # Try to write to protected registers
       try:
           dev.bank.regs.wdogload = 0x12345678
           # If we get here, the write succeeded, which is incorrect when locked
           print("Write to WDOGLOAD succeeded when locked (unexpected)")
       except:
           # Write failed as expected
           print("Write to WDOGLOAD failed when locked (expected)")
       try:
           dev.bank.regs.wdogcontrol = 0x3
           # If we get here, the write succeeded, which is incorrect when locked
           print("Write to WDOGCONTROL succeeded when locked (unexpected)")
       except:
           # Write failed as expected
           print("Write to WDOGCONTROL failed when locked (expected)")
   def test_unlock_sequence():
       print("Testing unlock sequence")
       # Test the correct unlock sequence
       unlock_key = 0x1ACCE551
       dev.bank.regs.wdoglock = unlock_key
       # After unlocking, writes should succeed
       try:
           dev.bank.regs.wdogload = 0x12345678
           print("Write to WDOGLOAD succeeded when unlocked (expected)")
       except:
           # Write failed, which is incorrect when unlocked
           print("Write to WDOGLOAD failed when unlocked (unexpected)")
       try:
           dev.bank.regs.wdogcontrol = 0x3
           print("Write to WDOGCONTROL succeeded when unlocked (expected)")
       except:
           # Write failed, which is incorrect when unlocked
           print("Write to WDOGCONTROL failed when unlocked (unexpected)")
   def test_lock_sequence():
       print("Testing lock sequence")
       # First unlock
       unlock_key = 0x1ACCE551
       dev.bank.regs.wdoglock = unlock_key
       # Then lock with any other value
       dev.bank.regs.wdoglock = 0x0
       # After locking, writes should fail again
       try:
           dev.bank.regs.wdogload = 0x87654321
           # If we get here, the write succeeded, which is incorrect when locked
           print("Write to WDOGLOAD succeeded when re-locked (unexpected)")
       except:
           # Write failed as expected
           print("Write to WDOGLOAD failed when re-locked (expected)")
   def test_invalid_unlock_keys():
       print("Testing invalid unlock keys")
       # Try various invalid unlock keys
       invalid_keys = [0x0, 0x1, 0xFFFFFFFF, 0x1ACCE550, 0x1ACCE552]
       for key in invalid_keys:
           # Device should remain locked
           dev.bank.regs.wdoglock = key
           # Try to write to protected register
           try:
               dev.bank.regs.wdogload = 0x12345678
               # If we get here, the write succeeded, which is incorrect
               print(f"Write succeeded with invalid key 0x{key:08x} (unexpected)")
           except:
               # Write failed as expected
               print(f"Write failed with invalid key 0x{key:08x} (expected)")
   # Run all tests
   test_initial_lock_state()
   test_write_protection_when_locked()
   test_unlock_sequence()
   test_lock_sequence()
   test_invalid_unlock_keys()
   print("All register protection tests completed."))

   Now I'll create the utility.dml file:
   🔧 write_file(file_path=./simics-project/modules/wdt/utility.dml, content=/* © 2024 Intel Corporation
    *
    * This software and the related documents are Intel copyrighted materials, and
    * your use of them is governed by the express license under which they were
    * provided to you ("License"). Unless the License provides otherwise, you may
    * not use, modify, copy, publish, distribute, disclose or transmit this software
    * or the related documents without Intel's prior written permission.
    *
    * This software and the related documents are provided as is, with no express or
    * implied warranties, other than those that are expressly stated in the License.
    */
   // Utility methods for the WDT device
   dml 1.4;
   // Utility method to check if device is locked
   method is_locked() -> (bool) {
       return (regs.wdoglock != 0x1ACCE551);
   }
   // Utility method to check if integration test mode is enabled
   method is_integration_test_mode() -> (bool) {
       return (regs.wdogitcr == 1);
   }
   // Utility method to update masked interrupt status
   method update_masked_interrupt_status() {
       regs.wdogmis = regs.wdogris & regs.wdogcontrol.int_en;
   }
   // Utility method to handle timer expiration
   method handle_timer_expiration() {
       // Check if interrupt is enabled
       if (regs.wdogcontrol.int_en) {
           // Check if interrupt is already pending
           if (!regs.wdogris) {
               // Generate interrupt
               regs.wdogris = 1;
               update_masked_interrupt_status();
               // Raise interrupt signal
               if (irq.obj) {
                   irq.signal.signal_raise();
               }
           } else if (regs.wdogcontrol.res_en) {
               // Second timeout with reset enabled - generate reset
               if (reset.obj) {
                   reset.signal.signal_raise();
               }
           }
       } else if (regs.wdogcontrol.res_en) {
           // Interrupt not enabled but reset is - generate reset immediately
           if (reset.obj) {
               reset.signal.signal_raise();
           }
       }
       // Reload the timer
       regs.wdogvalue = regs.wdogload;
   }
   // Utility method to clear interrupt
   method clear_interrupt() {
       regs.wdogris = 0;
       update_masked_interrupt_status();
       // Lower interrupt signal if it was raised
       if (irq.obj) {
           irq.signal.signal_lower();
       }
   }
   // Utility method to initialize ID registers
   method initialize_id_registers() {
       // Initialize Peripheral ID registers
       regs.wdogperiphid0 = 0x00000000;  // Placeholder values
       regs.wdogperiphid1 = 0x00000000;
       regs.wdogperiphid2 = 0x00000000;
       regs.wdogperiphid3 = 0x00000000;
       // Initialize PrimeCell ID registers
       regs.wdogpcellid0 = 0x00000000;   // Placeholder values
       regs.wdogpcellid1 = 0x00000000;
       regs.wdogpcellid2 = 0x00000000;
       regs.wdogpcellid3 = 0x00000000;
   })
   🆔 Invocation: e-b30327cd-4009-4dc2-b09a-da32d0a7cfe1

   // WDT.dml - Watchdog Timer Device Model
   dml 1.4;
   device wdt;
   param desc = "Watchdog Timer";
   param documentation = "32-bit decrementing watchdog timer with interrupt and reset generation capabilities";
   import "utility.dml";
   import "registers.dml";
   import "interfaces.dml";
   import "simics/devs/signal.dml";
   // Timer event declaration
   event timer_event "Event for timer expiration";
   // Saved variables for device state
   saved uint32 counter = 0;
   saved bool interrupt_pending = false;
   saved bool reset_pending = false;
   // Bank implementation with register behavior
   bank regs {
       param register_size = 4;
       // WDOGLOAD register implementation
       register wdogload {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogload.value;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGLOAD ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               regs.wdogload.value = value;
               log info: "WDOGLOAD set to 0x%08x", value;
           }
       }
       // WDOGVALUE register implementation
       register wdogvalue {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogvalue.value;
           }
       }
       // WDOGCONTROL register implementation
       register wdogcontrol {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return (regs.wdogcontrol.res_en << 1) | regs.wdogcontrol.int_en;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGCONTROL ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               regs.wdogcontrol.int_en = value & 0x1;
               regs.wdogcontrol.res_en = (value >> 1) & 0x1;
               update_masked_interrupt_status();
               log info: "WDOGCONTROL set to int_en=%d, res_en=%d", 
                         regs.wdogcontrol.int_en, regs.wdogcontrol.res_en;
           }
       }
       // WDOGINTCLR register implementation
       register wdogintclr {
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGINTCLR ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               clear_interrupt();
               log info: "Interrupt cleared";
           }
       }
       // WDOGRIS register implementation
       register wdogris {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogris.ris;
           }
       }
       // WDOGMIS register implementation
       register wdogmis {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogmis.mis;
           }
       }
       // WDOGLOCK register implementation
       register wdoglock {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdoglock.lock;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               if (value == 0x1ACCE551) {
                   regs.wdoglock.lock = value;
                   log info: "Device unlocked";
               } else {
                   regs.wdoglock.lock = value;
                   log info: "Device locked";
               }
           }
       }
       // WDOGITCR register implementation
       register wdogitcr {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogitcr.itcr;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGITCR ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               regs.wdogitcr.itcr = value & 0x1;
               log info: "Integration test mode set to %d", regs.wdogitcr.itcr;
           }
       }
       // WDOGITOP register implementation
       register wdogitop {
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGITOP ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               // Check if integration test mode is enabled
               if (is_integration_test_mode()) {
                   // Set interrupt output directly
                   if (value & 0x1) {
                       if (irq.obj) {
                           irq.signal.signal_raise();
                       }
                   } else {
                       if (irq.obj) {
                           irq.signal.signal_lower();
                       }
                   }
                   // Set reset output directly
                   if (value & 0x2) {
                       if (reset.obj) {
                           reset.signal.signal_raise();
                       }
                   } else {
                       if (reset.obj) {
                           reset.signal.signal_lower();
                       }
                   }
                   log info: "Direct output control: int=%d, res=%d", 
                             value & 0x1, (value >> 1) & 0x1;
               }
           }
       }
       // ID Register implementations
       register wdogperiphid0 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogperiphid0.pid0;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid1 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogperiphid1.pid1;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid2 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogperiphid2.pid2;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid3 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogperiphid3.pid3;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid0 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogpcellid0.cid0;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid1 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogpcellid1.cid1;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid2 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogpcellid2.cid2;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid3 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogpcellid3.cid3;
           }
       }
   }
   // Timer event implementation
   event timer_event {
       method handle() {
           // Check if integration test mode is enabled
           if (is_integration_test_mode()) {
               // In integration test mode, timer should not decrement
               return;
           }
           // Decrement the counter
           if (regs.wdogvalue.value > 0) {
               regs.wdogvalue.value--;
           }
           // Check if timer has expired
           if (regs.wdogvalue.value == 0) {
               handle_timer_expiration();
           } else {
               // Reschedule the timer event for next cycle
               after 1 cycles: timer_event;
           }
       }
   }
   // Initialization methods
   method init() {
       // Initialize register values
       regs.wdogload.value = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogvalue.value = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogcontrol.int_en = 0;
       regs.wdogcontrol.res_en = 0;
       regs.wdogris.ris = 0;
       regs.wdogmis.mis = 0;
       regs.wdoglock.lock = 0;  // Locked by default
       regs.wdogitcr.itcr = 0;
       // Initialize ID registers
       initialize_id_registers();
       log info: "WDT device initialized";
   }
   method post_init() {
       // Start the timer if clock is enabled
       // For now, we'll start the timer event
       after 1 cycles: timer_event;
       log info: "WDT device post-initialized";
   })
   🆔 Invocation: e-b30327cd-4009-4dc2-b09a-da32d0a7cfe1

           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogperiphid1.pid1;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid2 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogperiphid2.pid2;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid3 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogperiphid3.pid3;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid0 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogpcellid0.cid0;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid1 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogpcellid1.cid1;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid2 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogpcellid2.cid2;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid3 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return regs.wdogpcellid3.cid3;
           }
       }
   }
   // Timer event implementation
   event timer_event {
       method handle() {
           // Check if integration test mode is enabled
           if (is_integration_test_mode()) {
               // In integration test mode, timer should not decrement
               return;
           }
           // Decrement the counter
           if (regs.wdogvalue.value > 0) {
               regs.wdogvalue.value--;
           }
           // Check if timer has expired
           if (regs.wdogvalue.value == 0) {
               handle_timer_expiration();
           } else {
               // Reschedule the timer event for next cycle
               after 1 cycles: timer_event;
           }
       }
   }
   // Initialization methods
   method init() {
       // Initialize register values
       regs.wdogload.value = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogvalue.value = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogcontrol.int_en = 0;
       regs.wdogcontrol.res_en = 0;
       regs.wdogris.ris = 0;
       regs.wdogmis.mis = 0;
       regs.wdoglock.lock = 0;  // Locked by default
       regs.wdogitcr.itcr = 0;
       // Initialize ID registers
       initialize_id_registers();
       log info: "WDT device initialized";
   }
   method post_init() {
       // Start the timer if clock is enabled
       // For now, we'll start the timer event
       after 1 cycles: timer_event;
       log info: "WDT device post-initialized";
   }, overwrite=True)

   🔧 write_file(file_path=./simics-project/modules/wdt/wdt.dml, content=/*
     © 2024 Intel Corporation
   */
   // WDT.dml - Watchdog Timer Device Model
   dml 1.4;
   device wdt;
   param desc = "Watchdog Timer";
   param documentation = "32-bit decrementing watchdog timer with interrupt and reset generation capabilities";
   import "utility.dml";
   import "registers.dml";
   import "interfaces.dml";
   import "simics/devs/signal.dml";
   import "simics/devs/interrupt.dml";
   // Timer event declaration
   event timer_event is simple_time_event "Event for timer expiration";
   // Saved variables for device state
   saved uint32 counter = 0;
   saved bool interrupt_pending = false;
   saved bool reset_pending = false;
   // Attributes for interrupt and reset connections
   attribute irq_level is int64_attr {
       param configuration = "required";
   }
   // Bank implementation with register behavior
   bank regs {
       param register_size = 4;
       // WDOGLOAD register implementation
       register wdogload {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGLOAD ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               log info: "WDOGLOAD set to 0x%08x", value;
           }
       }
       // WDOGVALUE register implementation
       register wdogvalue {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       // WDOGCONTROL register implementation
       register wdogcontrol {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGCONTROL ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               update_masked_interrupt_status();
               log info: "WDOGCONTROL set to 0x%08x", value;
           }
       }
       // WDOGINTCLR register implementation
       register wdogintclr {
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGINTCLR ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               clear_interrupt();
               log info: "Interrupt cleared";
           }
       }
       // WDOGRIS register implementation
       register wdogris {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       // WDOGMIS register implementation
       register wdogmis {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       // WDOGLOCK register implementation
       register wdoglock {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               this.val = value;
               if (value == 0x1ACCE551) {
                   log info: "Device unlocked";
               } else {
                   log info: "Device locked";
               }
           }
       }
       // WDOGITCR register implementation
       register wdogitcr {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGITCR ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               log info: "Integration test mode set to %d", value);
           }
       }
       // WDOGITOP register implementation
       register wdogitop {
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGITOP ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               // Check if integration test mode is enabled
               if (regs.wdogitcr.val == 1) {
                   // Set interrupt output directly
                   if (value & 0x1) {
                       if (irq_dev.obj) {
                           irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt(irq_level.val);
                       }
                   } else {
                       if (irq_dev.obj) {
                           irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt_clear(irq_level.val);
                       }
                   }
                   // Set reset output directly
                   if (value & 0x2) {
                       if (reset_dev.obj) {
                           reset_dev.signal.signal_raise();
                       }
                   } else {
                       if (reset_dev.obj) {
                           reset_dev.signal.signal_lower();
                       }
                   }
                   log info: "Direct output control: int=%d, res=%d", 
                             value & 0x1, (value >> 1) & 0x1;
               }
           }
       }
       // ID Register implementations
       register wdogperiphid0 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid1 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid2 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid3 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid0 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid1 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid2 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid3 {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
   }
   // Timer event implementation
   event timer_event {
       method event() {
           // Check if integration test mode is enabled
           if (regs.wdogitcr.val == 1) {
               // In integration test mode, timer should not decrement
               return;
           }
           // Decrement the counter
           if (regs.wdogvalue.val > 0) {
               regs.wdogvalue.val--;
           }
           // Check if timer has expired
           if (regs.wdogvalue.val == 0) {
               handle_timer_expiration();
           } else {
               // Reschedule the timer event for next cycle
               post(1.0);
           }
       }
   }
   // Initialization methods
   method init() {
       // Initialize register values
       regs.wdogload.val = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogvalue.val = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogcontrol.val = 0;
       regs.wdogris.val = 0;
       regs.wdogmis.val = 0;
       regs.wdoglock.val = 0;  // Locked by default
       regs.wdogitcr.val = 0;
       // Initialize ID registers
       initialize_id_registers();
       log info: "WDT device initialized";
   }
   method post_init() {
       // Start the timer if clock is enabled
       // For now, we'll start the timer event
       timer_event.post(1.0);
       log info: "WDT device post-initialized";
   }, overwrite=True)

   Let me simplify the approach and put all the register definitions directly in the main wdt.dml file, removing the separate registers.dml and interfaces.dml files for now:
   🔧 write_file(file_path=./simics-project/modules/wdt/wdt.dml, content=/*
     © 2024 Intel Corporation
   */
   // WDT.dml - Watchdog Timer Device Model
   dml 1.4;
   device wdt;
   param desc = "Watchdog Timer";
   param documentation = "32-bit decrementing watchdog timer with interrupt and reset generation capabilities";
   import "utility.dml";
   import "simics/devs/signal.dml";
   import "simics/devs/interrupt.dml";
   // Timer event declaration
   event timer_event is simple_time_event "Event for timer expiration";
   // Saved variables for device state
   saved uint32 counter = 0;
   saved bool interrupt_pending = false;
   saved bool reset_pending = false;
   // Attributes for interrupt and reset connections
   attribute irq_level is int64_attr {
       param configuration = "required";
   }
   // Interrupt output connection
   connect irq_dev {
       interface simple_interrupt;
   }
   // Reset output connection
   connect reset_dev {
       interface signal;
   }
   // Bank implementation with register behavior
   bank regs {
       param register_size = 4;
       // Control Registers
       register wdogload @ 0x0000 is (read, write) "Load value register" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGLOAD ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               log info: "WDOGLOAD set to 0x%08x", value;
           }
       }
       register wdogvalue @ 0x0004 is (read) "Current counter value" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogcontrol @ 0x0008 is (read, write) "Control register" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGCONTROL ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               update_masked_interrupt_status();
               log info: "WDOGCONTROL set to 0x%08x", value;
           }
       }
       register wdogintclr @ 0x000C is (write) "Interrupt clear register" {
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGINTCLR ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               clear_interrupt();
               log info: "Interrupt cleared";
           }
       }
       register wdogris @ 0x0010 is (read) "Raw interrupt status" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogmis @ 0x0014 is (read) "Masked interrupt status" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdoglock @ 0x0C00 is (read, write) "Lock register" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               this.val = value;
               if (value == 0x1ACCE551) {
                   log info: "Device unlocked";
               } else {
                   log info: "Device locked";
               }
           }
       }
       // Integration Test Registers
       register wdogitcr @ 0x0F00 is (read, write) "Integration test control" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGITCR ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               log info: "Integration test mode set to %d", value;
           }
       }
       register wdogitop @ 0x0F04 is (write) "Integration test output set" {
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGITOP ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               // Check if integration test mode is enabled
               if (regs.wdogitcr.val == 1) {
                   // Set interrupt output directly
                   if (value & 0x1) {
                       if (irq_dev.obj) {
                           irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt(irq_level.val);
                       }
                   } else {
                       if (irq_dev.obj) {
                           irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt_clear(irq_level.val);
                       }
                   }
                   // Set reset output directly
                   if (value & 0x2) {
                       if (reset_dev.obj) {
                           reset_dev.signal.signal_raise();
                       }
                   } else {
                       if (reset_dev.obj) {
                           reset_dev.signal.signal_lower();
                       }
                   }
                   log info: "Direct output control: int=%d, res=%d", 
                             value & 0x1, (value >> 1) & 0x1;
               }
           }
       }
       // ID Registers
       register wdogperiphid0 @ 0x0FE0 is (read) "Peripheral ID register 0" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid1 @ 0x0FE4 is (read) "Peripheral ID register 1" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid2 @ 0x0FE8 is (read) "Peripheral ID register 2" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid3 @ 0x0FEC is (read) "Peripheral ID register 3" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid0 @ 0x0FF0 is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 0" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid1 @ 0x0FF4 is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 1" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid2 @ 0x0FF8 is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 2" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid3 @ 0x0FFC is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 3" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
   }
   // Timer event implementation
   event timer_event {
       method event() {
           // Check if integration test mode is enabled
           if (regs.wdogitcr.val == 1) {
               // In integration test mode, timer should not decrement
               return;
           }
           // Decrement the counter
           if (regs.wdogvalue.val > 0) {
               regs.wdogvalue.val--;
           }
           // Check if timer has expired
           if (regs.wdogvalue.val == 0) {
               handle_timer_expiration();
           } else {
               // Reschedule the timer event for next cycle
               post(1.0);
           }
       }
   }
   // Initialization methods
   method init() {
       // Initialize register values
       regs.wdogload.val = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogvalue.val = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogcontrol.val = 0;
       regs.wdogris.val = 0;
       regs.wdogmis.val = 0;
       regs.wdoglock.val = 0;  // Locked by default
       regs.wdogitcr.val = 0;
       // Initialize ID registers
       initialize_id_registers();
       log info: "WDT device initialized";
   }
   method post_init() {
       // Start the timer if clock is enabled
       // For now, we'll start the timer event
       timer_event.post(1.0);
       log info: "WDT device post-initialized";
   }, overwrite=True)

   // WDT.dml - Watchdog Timer Device Model
   dml 1.4;
   device wdt;
   param desc = "Watchdog Timer";
   param documentation = "32-bit decrementing watchdog timer with interrupt and reset generation capabilities";
   import "simics/devs/signal.dml";
   import "simics/devs/interrupt.dml";
   // Timer event declaration
   event timer_event is simple_time_event "Event for timer expiration";
   // Saved variables for device state
   saved uint32 counter = 0;
   saved bool interrupt_pending = false;
   saved bool reset_pending = false;
   // Attributes for interrupt and reset connections
   attribute irq_level is int64_attr {
       param configuration = "required";
       param documentation = "The level at which interrupts are sent.";
   }
   // Interrupt output connection
   connect irq_dev {
       interface simple_interrupt;
   }
   // Reset output connection
   connect reset_dev {
       interface signal;
   }
   // Bank implementation with register behavior
   bank regs {
       param register_size = 4;
       // Control Registers
       register wdogload @ 0x0000 is (read, write) "Load value register" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGLOAD ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               log info: "WDOGLOAD set to 0x%08x", value;
           }
       }
       register wdogvalue @ 0x0004 is (read) "Current counter value" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogcontrol @ 0x0008 is (read, write) "Control register" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGCONTROL ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               update_masked_interrupt_status();
               log info: "WDOGCONTROL set to 0x%08x", value;
           }
       }
       register wdogintclr @ 0x000C is (write) "Interrupt clear register" {
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGINTCLR ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               clear_interrupt();
               log info: "Interrupt cleared";
           }
       }
       register wdogris @ 0x0010 is (read) "Raw interrupt status" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogmis @ 0x0014 is (read) "Masked interrupt status" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdoglock @ 0x0C00 is (read, write) "Lock register" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               this.val = value;
               if (value == 0x1ACCE551) {
                   log info: "Device unlocked";
               } else {
                   log info: "Device locked";
               }
           }
       }
       // Integration Test Registers
       register wdogitcr @ 0x0F00 is (read, write) "Integration test control" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGITCR ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               log info: "Integration test mode set to %d", value;
           }
       }
       register wdogitop @ 0x0F04 is (write) "Integration test output set" {
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGITOP ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               // Check if integration test mode is enabled
               if (regs.wdogitcr.val == 1) {
                   // Set interrupt output directly
                   if ((value & 0x1) != 0) {
                       if (irq_dev.obj) {
                           irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt(irq_level.val);
                       }
                   } else {
                       if (irq_dev.obj) {
                           irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt_clear(irq_level.val);
                       }
                   }
                   // Set reset output directly
                   if ((value & 0x2) != 0) {
                       if (reset_dev.obj) {
                           reset_dev.signal.signal_raise();
                       }
                   } else {
                       if (reset_dev.obj) {
                           reset_dev.signal.signal_lower();
                       }
                   }
                   log info: "Direct output control: int=%d, res=%d", 
                             value & 0x1, (value >> 1) & 0x1;
               }
           }
       }
       // ID Registers
       register wdogperiphid0 @ 0x0FE0 is (read) "Peripheral ID register 0" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid1 @ 0x0FE4 is (read) "Peripheral ID register 1" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid2 @ 0x0FE8 is (read) "Peripheral ID register 2" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid3 @ 0x0FEC is (read) "Peripheral ID register 3" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid0 @ 0x0FF0 is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 0" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid1 @ 0x0FF4 is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 1" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid2 @ 0x0FF8 is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 2" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid3 @ 0x0FFC is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 3" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
   }
   // Timer event implementation
   event timer_event {
       method event() {
           // Check if integration test mode is enabled
           if (regs.wdogitcr.val == 1) {
               // In integration test mode, timer should not decrement
               return;
           }
           // Decrement the counter
           if (regs.wdogvalue.val > 0) {
               regs.wdogvalue.val--;
           }
           // Check if timer has expired
           if (regs.wdogvalue.val == 0) {
               handle_timer_expiration();
           } else {
               // Reschedule the timer event for next cycle
               post(1.0);
           }
       }
   }
   // Utility method to check if device is locked
   method is_locked() -> (bool) {
       return (regs.wdoglock.val != 0x1ACCE551);
   }
   // Utility method to update masked interrupt status
   method update_masked_interrupt_status() {
       regs.wdogmis.val = regs.wdogris.val & (regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x1);
   }
   // Utility method to handle timer expiration
   method handle_timer_expiration() {
       // Check if interrupt is enabled
       if ((regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x1) != 0) {
           // Check if interrupt is already pending
           if (regs.wdogris.val == 0) {
               // Generate interrupt
               regs.wdogris.val = 1;
               update_masked_interrupt_status();
               // Raise interrupt signal
               if (irq_dev.obj) {
                   irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt(irq_level.val);
               }
           } else if ((regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x2) != 0) {
               // Second timeout with reset enabled - generate reset
               if (reset_dev.obj) {
                   reset_dev.signal.signal_raise();
               }
           }
       } else if ((regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x2) != 0) {
           // Interrupt not enabled but reset is - generate reset immediately
           if (reset_dev.obj) {
               reset_dev.signal.signal_raise();
           }
       }
       // Reload the timer
       regs.wdogvalue.val = regs.wdogload.val;
   }
   // Utility method to clear interrupt
   method clear_interrupt() {
       regs.wdogris.val = 0;
       update_masked_interrupt_status();
       // Lower interrupt signal if it was raised
       if (irq_dev.obj) {
           irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt_clear(irq_level.val);
       }
   }
   // Utility method to initialize ID registers
   method initialize_id_registers() {
       // Initialize Peripheral ID registers
       regs.wdogperiphid0.val = 0x00000000;  // Placeholder values
       regs.wdogperiphid1.val = 0x00000000;
       regs.wdogperiphid2.val = 0x00000000;
       regs.wdogperiphid3.val = 0x00000000;
       // Initialize PrimeCell ID registers
       regs.wdogpcellid0.val = 0x00000000;   // Placeholder values
       regs.wdogpcellid1.val = 0x00000000;
       regs.wdogpcellid2.val = 0x00000000;
       regs.wdogpcellid3.val = 0x00000000;
   }
   // Initialization methods
   method init() {
       // Initialize register values
       regs.wdogload.val = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogvalue.val = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogcontrol.val = 0;
       regs.wdogris.val = 0;
       regs.wdogmis.val = 0;
       regs.wdoglock.val = 0;  // Locked by default
       regs.wdogitcr.val = 0;
       // Initialize ID registers
       initialize_id_registers();
       log info: "WDT device initialized";
   }
   method post_init() {
       // Start the timer if clock is enabled
       // For now, we'll start the timer event
       timer_event.post(1.0);
       log info: "WDT device post-initialized";
   }, overwrite=True)

   🔧 write_file(file_path=./simics-project/modules/wdt/test/wdt_common.py, content=# © 2010 Intel Corporation
   import simics
   # Extend this function if your device requires any additional attributes to be
   # set. It is often sensible to make additional arguments to this function
   # optional, and let the function create mock objects if needed.
   def create_wdt(name = None):
       '''Create a new wdt object'''
       wdt = simics.pre_conf_object(name, 'wdt')
       wdt.irq_level = 0  # Set the required irq_level attribute
       simics.SIM_add_configuration([wdt], None)
       return simics.SIM_get_object(wdt.name), overwrite=True)

   # Extend this function if your device requires any additional attributes to be
   # set. It is often sensible to make additional arguments to this function
   # optional, and let the function create mock objects if needed.
   def create_wdt(name = None):
       '''Create a new wdt object'''
       wdt = simics.pre_conf_object(name, 'wdt')
       wdt.irq_level = 0  # Set the required irq_level attribute
       # Create a clock for the queue attribute
       clock = simics.pre_conf_object('clock', 'clock')
       clock.freq_mhz = 1000  # 1 GHz clock
       wdt.queue = clock
       simics.SIM_add_configuration([clock, wdt], None)
       return simics.SIM_get_object(wdt.name), overwrite=True)

[92m🤖 [implement_agent] [0m
   Now let me fix the register protection issue in the wdt.dml file. The problem is that the is_locked() method is not working correctly. Let me update it:
   🔧 write_file(file_path=./simics-project/modules/wdt/wdt.dml, content=/*
     © 2024 Intel Corporation
   */
   // WDT.dml - Watchdog Timer Device Model
   dml 1.4;
   device wdt;
   param desc = "Watchdog Timer";
   param documentation = "32-bit decrementing watchdog timer with interrupt and reset generation capabilities";
   import "simics/devs/signal.dml";
   import "simics/devs/interrupt.dml";
   // Timer event declaration
   event timer_event is simple_time_event "Event for timer expiration";
   // Saved variables for device state
   saved uint32 counter = 0;
   saved bool interrupt_pending = false;
   saved bool reset_pending = false;
   // Attributes for interrupt and reset connections
   attribute irq_level is int64_attr {
       param configuration = "required";
       param documentation = "The level at which interrupts are sent.";
   }
   // Interrupt output connection
   connect irq_dev {
       interface simple_interrupt;
   }
   // Reset output connection
   connect reset_dev {
       interface signal;
   }
   // Bank implementation with register behavior
   bank regs {
       param register_size = 4;
       // Control Registers
       register wdogload @ 0x0000 is (read, write) "Load value register" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGLOAD ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               log info: "WDOGLOAD set to 0x%08x", value;
           }
       }
       register wdogvalue @ 0x0004 is (read) "Current counter value" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogcontrol @ 0x0008 is (read, write) "Control register" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGCONTROL ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               update_masked_interrupt_status();
               log info: "WDOGCONTROL set to 0x%08x", value;
           }
       }
       register wdogintclr @ 0x000C is (write) "Interrupt clear register" {
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGINTCLR ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               clear_interrupt();
               log info: "Interrupt cleared";
           }
       }
       register wdogris @ 0x0010 is (read) "Raw interrupt status" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogmis @ 0x0014 is (read) "Masked interrupt status" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdoglock @ 0x0C00 is (read, write) "Lock register" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               this.val = value;
               if (value == 0x1ACCE551) {
                   log info: "Device unlocked";
               } else {
                   log info: "Device locked";
               }
           }
       }
       // Integration Test Registers
       register wdogitcr @ 0x0F00 is (read, write) "Integration test control" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGITCR ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               log info: "Integration test mode set to %d", value;
           }
       }
       register wdogitop @ 0x0F04 is (write) "Integration test output set" {
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGITOP ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               // Check if integration test mode is enabled
               if (regs.wdogitcr.val == 1) {
                   // Set interrupt output directly
                   if ((value & 0x1) != 0) {
                       if (irq_dev.obj) {
                           irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt(irq_level.val);
                       }
                   } else {
                       if (irq_dev.obj) {
                           irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt_clear(irq_level.val);
                       }
                   }
                   // Set reset output directly
                   if ((value & 0x2) != 0) {
                       if (reset_dev.obj) {
                           reset_dev.signal.signal_raise();
                       }
                   } else {
                       if (reset_dev.obj) {
                           reset_dev.signal.signal_lower();
                       }
                   }
                   log info: "Direct output control: int=%d, res=%d", 
                             value & 0x1, (value >> 1) & 0x1;
               }
           }
       }
       // ID Registers
       register wdogperiphid0 @ 0x0FE0 is (read) "Peripheral ID register 0" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid1 @ 0x0FE4 is (read) "Peripheral ID register 1" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid2 @ 0x0FE8 is (read) "Peripheral ID register 2" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid3 @ 0x0FEC is (read) "Peripheral ID register 3" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid0 @ 0x0FF0 is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 0" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid1 @ 0x0FF4 is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 1" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid2 @ 0x0FF8 is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 2" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid3 @ 0x0FFC is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 3" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
   }
   // Timer event implementation
   event timer_event {
       method event() {
           // Check if integration test mode is enabled
           if (regs.wdogitcr.val == 1) {
               // In integration test mode, timer should not decrement
               return;
           }
           // Decrement the counter
           if (regs.wdogvalue.val > 0) {
               regs.wdogvalue.val--;
           }
           // Check if timer has expired
           if (regs.wdogvalue.val == 0) {
               handle_timer_expiration();
           } else {
               // Reschedule the timer event for next cycle
               post(1.0);
           }
       }
   }
   // Utility method to check if device is locked
   method is_locked() -> (bool) {
       return (regs.wdoglock.val != 0x1ACCE551);
   }
   // Utility method to update masked interrupt status
   method update_masked_interrupt_status() {
       regs.wdogmis.val = regs.wdogris.val & (regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x1);
   }
   // Utility method to handle timer expiration
   method handle_timer_expiration() {
       // Check if interrupt is enabled
       if ((regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x1) != 0) {
           // Check if interrupt is already pending
           if (regs.wdogris.val == 0) {
               // Generate interrupt
               regs.wdogris.val = 1;
               update_masked_interrupt_status();
               // Raise interrupt signal
               if (irq_dev.obj) {
                   irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt(irq_level.val);
               }
           } else if ((regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x2) != 0) {
               // Second timeout with reset enabled - generate reset
               if (reset_dev.obj) {
                   reset_dev.signal.signal_raise();
               }
           }
       } else if ((regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x2) != 0) {
           // Interrupt not enabled but reset is - generate reset immediately
           if (reset_dev.obj) {
               reset_dev.signal.signal_raise();
           }
       }
       // Reload the timer
       regs.wdogvalue.val = regs.wdogload.val;
   }
   // Utility method to clear interrupt
   method clear_interrupt() {
       regs.wdogris.val = 0;
       update_masked_interrupt_status();
       // Lower interrupt signal if it was raised
       if (irq_dev.obj) {
           irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt_clear(irq_level.val);
       }
   }
   // Utility method to initialize ID registers
   method initialize_id_registers() {
       // Initialize Peripheral ID registers with ARM values
       regs.wdogperiphid0.val = 0x00000024;  // ARM watchdog ID
       regs.wdogperiphid1.val = 0x00000018;
       regs.wdogperiphid2.val = 0x00000004;
       regs.wdogperiphid3.val = 0x00000000;
       // Initialize PrimeCell ID registers
       regs.wdogpcellid0.val = 0x0000000D;
       regs.wdogpcellid1.val = 0x000000F0;
       regs.wdogpcellid2.val = 0x00000005;
       regs.wdogpcellid3.val = 0x000000B1;
   }
   // Initialization methods
   method init() {
       // Initialize register values
       regs.wdogload.val = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogvalue.val = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogcontrol.val = 0;
       regs.wdogris.val = 0;
       regs.wdogmis.val = 0;
       regs.wdoglock.val = 0;  // Locked by default
       regs.wdogitcr.val = 0;
       // Initialize ID registers
       initialize_id_registers();
       log info: "WDT device initialized";
   }
   method post_init() {
       // Start the timer if clock is enabled
       // For now, we'll start the timer event
       timer_event.post(1.0);
       log info: "WDT device post-initialized";
   }, overwrite=True)
   🆔 Invocation: e-b30327cd-4009-4dc2-b09a-da32d0a7cfe1

[92m🤖 [implement_agent] [0m
   I can see the issue. In the `test_wdogvalue()` function, it's expecting that when you set `wdogload` to `0x1000`, the `wdogvalue` should also be `0x1000`. But in our implementation, we're initializing `wdogvalue` to `0xFFFFFFFF` and it's not being updated when `wdogload` is set.
   Let me fix this by updating the `wdogload` register write method to also update `wdogvalue`:
   🔧 write_file(file_path=./simics-project/modules/wdt/wdt.dml, content=/*
     © 2024 Intel Corporation
   */
   // WDT.dml - Watchdog Timer Device Model
   dml 1.4;
   device wdt;
   param desc = "Watchdog Timer";
   param documentation = "32-bit decrementing watchdog timer with interrupt and reset generation capabilities";
   import "simics/devs/signal.dml";
   import "simics/devs/interrupt.dml";
   // Timer event declaration
   event timer_event is simple_time_event "Event for timer expiration";
   // Saved variables for device state
   saved uint32 counter = 0;
   saved bool interrupt_pending = false;
   saved bool reset_pending = false;
   // Attributes for interrupt and reset connections
   attribute irq_level is int64_attr {
       param configuration = "required";
       param documentation = "The level at which interrupts are sent.";
   }
   // Interrupt output connection
   connect irq_dev {
       interface simple_interrupt;
   }
   // Reset output connection
   connect reset_dev {
       interface signal;
   }
   // Bank implementation with register behavior
   bank regs {
       param register_size = 4;
       // Control Registers
       register wdogload @ 0x0000 is (read, write) "Load value register" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGLOAD ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               // Also update the current value register
               regs.wdogvalue.val = value;
               log info: "WDOGLOAD set to 0x%08x, WDOGVALUE updated to 0x%08x", value, value;
           }
       }
       register wdogvalue @ 0x0004 is (read) "Current counter value" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogcontrol @ 0x0008 is (read, write) "Control register" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGCONTROL ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               update_masked_interrupt_status();
               log info: "WDOGCONTROL set to 0x%08x", value;
           }
       }
       register wdogintclr @ 0x000C is (write) "Interrupt clear register" {
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGINTCLR ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               clear_interrupt();
               log info: "Interrupt cleared";
           }
       }
       register wdogris @ 0x0010 is (read) "Raw interrupt status" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogmis @ 0x0014 is (read) "Masked interrupt status" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdoglock @ 0x0C00 is (read, write) "Lock register" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               this.val = value;
               if (value == 0x1ACCE551) {
                   log info: "Device unlocked";
               } else {
                   log info: "Device locked";
               }
           }
       }
       // Integration Test Registers
       register wdogitcr @ 0x0F00 is (read, write) "Integration test control" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGITCR ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               this.val = value;
               log info: "Integration test mode set to %d", value;
           }
       }
       register wdogitop @ 0x0F04 is (write) "Integration test output set" {
           method write(uint64 value) {
               // Check if device is locked
               if (is_locked()) {
                   log info: "Write to WDOGITOP ignored - device locked";
                   return;
               }
               // Check if integration test mode is enabled
               if (regs.wdogitcr.val == 1) {
                   // Set interrupt output directly
                   if ((value & 0x1) != 0) {
                       if (irq_dev.obj) {
                           irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt(irq_level.val);
                       }
                   } else {
                       if (irq_dev.obj) {
                           irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt_clear(irq_level.val);
                       }
                   }
                   // Set reset output directly
                   if ((value & 0x2) != 0) {
                       if (reset_dev.obj) {
                           reset_dev.signal.signal_raise();
                       }
                   } else {
                       if (reset_dev.obj) {
                           reset_dev.signal.signal_lower();
                       }
                   }
                   log info: "Direct output control: int=%d, res=%d", 
                             value & 0x1, (value >> 1) & 0x1;
               }
           }
       }
       // ID Registers
       register wdogperiphid0 @ 0x0FE0 is (read) "Peripheral ID register 0" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid1 @ 0x0FE4 is (read) "Peripheral ID register 1" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid2 @ 0x0FE8 is (read) "Peripheral ID register 2" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogperiphid3 @ 0x0FEC is (read) "Peripheral ID register 3" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid0 @ 0x0FF0 is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 0" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid1 @ 0x0FF4 is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 1" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid2 @ 0x0FF8 is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 2" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
       register wdogpcellid3 @ 0x0FFC is (read) "PrimeCell ID register 3" {
           method read() -> (uint64) {
               return this.val;
           }
       }
   }
   // Timer event implementation
   event timer_event {
       method event() {
           // Check if integration test mode is enabled
           if (regs.wdogitcr.val == 1) {
               // In integration test mode, timer should not decrement
               return;
           }
           // Decrement the counter
           if (regs.wdogvalue.val > 0) {
               regs.wdogvalue.val--;
           }
           // Check if timer has expired
           if (regs.wdogvalue.val == 0) {
               handle_timer_expiration();
           } else {
               // Reschedule the timer event for next cycle
               post(1.0);
           }
       }
   }
   // Utility method to check if device is locked
   method is_locked() -> (bool) {
       return (regs.wdoglock.val != 0x1ACCE551);
   }
   // Utility method to update masked interrupt status
   method update_masked_interrupt_status() {
       regs.wdogmis.val = regs.wdogris.val & (regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x1);
   }
   // Utility method to handle timer expiration
   method handle_timer_expiration() {
       // Check if interrupt is enabled
       if ((regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x1) != 0) {
           // Check if interrupt is already pending
           if (regs.wdogris.val == 0) {
               // Generate interrupt
               regs.wdogris.val = 1;
               update_masked_interrupt_status();
               // Raise interrupt signal
               if (irq_dev.obj) {
                   irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt(irq_level.val);
               }
           } else if ((regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x2) != 0) {
               // Second timeout with reset enabled - generate reset
               if (reset_dev.obj) {
                   reset_dev.signal.signal_raise();
               }
           }
       } else if ((regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x2) != 0) {
           // Interrupt not enabled but reset is - generate reset immediately
           if (reset_dev.obj) {
               reset_dev.signal.signal_raise();
           }
       }
       // Reload the timer
       regs.wdogvalue.val = regs.wdogload.val;
   }
   // Utility method to clear interrupt
   method clear_interrupt() {
       regs.wdogris.val = 0;
       update_masked_interrupt_status();
       // Lower interrupt signal if it was raised
       if (irq_dev.obj) {
           irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt_clear(irq_level.val);
       }
   }
   // Utility method to initialize ID registers
   method initialize_id_registers() {
       // Initialize Peripheral ID registers with ARM values
       regs.wdogperiphid0.val = 0x00000024;  // ARM watchdog ID
       regs.wdogperiphid1.val = 0x00000018;
       regs.wdogperiphid2.val = 0x00000004;
       regs.wdogperiphid3.val = 0x00000000;
       // Initialize PrimeCell ID registers
       regs.wdogpcellid0.val = 0x0000000D;
       regs.wdogpcellid1.val = 0x000000F0;
       regs.wdogpcellid2.val = 0x00000005;
       regs.wdogpcellid3.val = 0x000000B1;
   }
   // Initialization methods
   method init() {
       // Initialize register values
       regs.wdogload.val = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogvalue.val = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogcontrol.val = 0;
       regs.wdogris.val = 0;
       regs.wdogmis.val = 0;
       regs.wdoglock.val = 0;  // Locked by default
       regs.wdogitcr.val = 0;
       // Initialize ID registers
       initialize_id_registers();
       log info: "WDT device initialized";
   }
   method post_init() {
       // Start the timer if clock is enabled
       // For now, we'll start the timer event
       timer_event.post(1.0);
       log info: "WDT device post-initialized";
   }, overwrite=True)

   device wdt;
   param desc = "Watchdog Timer";
   param documentation = "32-bit decrementing watchdog timer with interrupt and reset generation capabilities";
   import "simics/devs/signal.dml";
   import "simics/devs/interrupt.dml";
   // Timer event declaration
   event timer_event is simple_time_event "Event for timer expiration";
   // Saved variables for device state

       param configuration = "required";

                   return;

               this.val = value;

           }

           }

           method read() -> (uint64) {
               log info: "Reading WDOGPCELLID3: 0x%08x", this.val;
               return this.val;
           }
       }
   }
   // Timer event implementation
   event timer_event {
       method event() {
           log info: "Timer event triggered, WDOGVALUE = 0x%08x", regs.wdogvalue.val;
           // Check if integration test mode is enabled
           if (regs.wdogitcr.val == 1) {
               // In integration test mode, timer should not decrement
               log info: "Integration test mode enabled, timer not decrementing";
               return;
           }
           // Decrement the counter
           if (regs.wdogvalue.val > 0) {
               regs.wdogvalue.val--;
               log info: "Timer decremented, WDOGVALUE = 0x%08x", regs.wdogvalue.val;
           }
           // Check if timer has expired
           if (regs.wdogvalue.val == 0) {
               log info: "Timer expired, handling expiration";
               handle_timer_expiration();
           } else {
               // Reschedule the timer event for next cycle
               log info: "Timer not expired, rescheduling";
               post(1.0);
           }
       }
   }
   // Utility method to check if device is locked
   method is_locked() -> (bool) {
       log info: "Checking if device is locked, WDOGLOCK = 0x%08x", regs.wdoglock.val;
       return (regs.wdoglock.val != 0x1ACCE551);
   }
   // Utility method to update masked interrupt status
   method update_masked_interrupt_status() {
       regs.wdogmis.val = regs.wdogris.val & (regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x1);
       log info: "Updated masked interrupt status, WDOGMIS = 0x%08x", regs.wdogmis.val;
   }
   // Utility method to handle timer expiration
   method handle_timer_expiration() {
       log info: "Handling timer expiration";
       // Check if interrupt is enabled
       if ((regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x1) != 0) {
           // Check if interrupt is already pending
           if (regs.wdogris.val == 0) {
               // Generate interrupt
               regs.wdogris.val = 1;
               update_masked_interrupt_status();
               // Raise interrupt signal
               if (irq_dev.obj) {
                   irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt(irq_level.val);
               }
               log info: "Generated interrupt";
           } else if ((regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x2) != 0) {
               // Second timeout with reset enabled - generate reset
               if (reset_dev.obj) {
                   reset_dev.signal.signal_raise();
               }
               log info: "Generated reset (second timeout)";
           }
       } else if ((regs.wdogcontrol.val & 0x2) != 0) {
           // Interrupt not enabled but reset is - generate reset immediately
           if (reset_dev.obj) {
               reset_dev.signal.signal_raise();
           }
           log info: "Generated reset (interrupt disabled)";
       }
       // Reload the timer
       regs.wdogvalue.val = regs.wdogload.val;
       log info: "Timer reloaded, WDOGVALUE = 0x%08x", regs.wdogvalue.val;
   }
   // Utility method to clear interrupt
   method clear_interrupt() {
       regs.wdogris.val = 0;
       update_masked_interrupt_status();
       // Lower interrupt signal if it was raised
       if (irq_dev.obj) {
           irq_dev.simple_interrupt.interrupt_clear(irq_level.val);
       }
       log info: "Interrupt cleared";
   }
   // Utility method to initialize ID registers
   method initialize_id_registers() {
       // Initialize Peripheral ID registers with ARM values
       regs.wdogperiphid0.val = 0x00000024;  // ARM watchdog ID
       regs.wdogperiphid1.val = 0x00000018;
       regs.wdogperiphid2.val = 0x00000004;
       regs.wdogperiphid3.val = 0x00000000;
       // Initialize PrimeCell ID registers
       regs.wdogpcellid0.val = 0x0000000D;
       regs.wdogpcellid1.val = 0x000000F0;
       regs.wdogpcellid2.val = 0x00000005;
       regs.wdogpcellid3.val = 0x000000B1;
       log info: "ID registers initialized";
   }
   // Initialization methods
   method init() {
       // Initialize register values
       regs.wdogload.val = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogvalue.val = 0xFFFFFFFF;
       regs.wdogcontrol.val = 0;
       regs.wdogris.val = 0;
       regs.wdogmis.val = 0;
       regs.wdoglock.val = 0;  // Locked by default
       regs.wdogitcr.val = 0;
       // Initialize ID registers
       initialize_id_registers();
       log info: "WDT device initialized";
   }
   method post_init() {
       // Start the timer if clock is enabled
       // For now, we'll start the timer event
